1b:[["$","section",null,{"className":"my-7","children":[["$","p",null,{"className":"text-[16.5px] leading-relaxed text-ink-secondary","children":"Chapter 3 removed state to isolate exploration. Now we put it back, and the whole subject changes character. This chapter derives it in full, proves the fixed-point machinery of Chapter 2 applies to it, and then confesses the thing most textbooks postpone: robots never see the state, so the honest formalism
